                                          Quick Salmon Deviled Eggs
Ingredients                    Directions
18 eggs                        Place the eggs into a saucepan in a single layer, and fill with cold
6 ounces smoked salmon cream   water to cover the eggs by at least 1 inch. Cover the saucepan, and
cheese spread                  bring the water to a boil over high heat. Remove from the heat, and
2 tablespoons mayonnaise       let the eggs stand in the hot water for 15 minutes. Remove eggs
paprika to taste               from water, and cool. Peel once cold.

                               Cut the eggs in half lengthwise. Place the egg yolks in a mixing
                               bowl, and set the whites aside. Mash the yolks with the cream
                               cheese and mayonnaise until thoroughly blended. Fill the egg white
                               halves with the egg yolk mixture using a spoon. Sprinkle with
                               paprika. Refrigerate until ready to serve.

                                                                             Skrie Salmon
Ingredients                       Directions
1/4 teaspoon anise seed           Preheat oven to 350 degrees F (175 degrees C).
1/8 teaspoon crushed dried mint
1 1/4 cups water, divided         In a small saucepan, stir together anise seeds and mint. Stir in 1
3/4 teaspoon honey                cup water, and bring to a boil over high heat. Boil 5 minutes, then
3 strips lemon peel               stir in honey. Continue to boil until most of the water has boiled off.
3 tablespoons butter, melted      Remove from heat, and stir in remaining 1/4 cup water.
2 pounds salmon fillets
                                  Place lemon strips in a bowl, and pour melted butter over the strips.
                                  Let sit 5 minutes.

                                  Place salmon on baking sheet. Brush thoroughly with anise sauce.

                                  Place in a preheated oven, and bake until done, about 15 minutes.
                                  Remove from oven, and brush liberally with lemon butter.

                              Seared Salmon with Balsamic Sauce
Ingredients                         Directions
4 (4 ounce) salmon fillets         Sprinkle both sides of fillets with salt and pepper. In a large nonstick
1/2 teaspoon salt                  skillet, cook salmon in oil over medium heat for 10-15 minutes or
1/4 teaspoon pepper                until fish flakes easily with a fork, turning once. Remove and keep
2 teaspoons canola oil             warm. Combine the water, vinegar, lemon juice and brown sugar;
1/4 cup water                      pour into skillet. Bring to a boil; cook until liquid is reduced to about
1/4 cup balsamic vinegar           1/3 cup. Serve over salmon.
4 1/2 teaspoons lemon juice
4 teaspoons brown sugar

                                                                             Paper Salmon
Ingredients                          Directions
2 (5 ounce) salmon fillets or steaks Preheat the oven to 425 degrees F (220 degrees C).
2 tablespoons frozen green peas
2 cloves garlic, crushed             Place each piece of salmon on a large (12 inch) circle of parchment
2 dashes lemon juice                 paper so that they are 1 inch from the center. Cover each with a
1 tablespoon olive oil               spoonful of peas, a clove of crushed garlic, a squeeze of lemon
                                     juice and a drizzle of olive oil. Fold the paper over into a packet and
                                     seal the edges by crimping and folding like a pasty. Place on a
                                     baking sheet.

                                     Bake for 15 minutes in the preheated oven, or until fish is able to
                                     flake with a fork. To serve, place the packets onto serving plates
                                     and cut open the center in the shape of a cross.

1 tablespoon grated fresh ginger     for at least 15 minutes, or up to one hour.
root
                                     Preheat an outdoor grill for medium heat. Place the planks on the
1 teaspoon minced garlic
                                     grate. The boards are ready when they start to smoke and crackle
2 (2 pound) salmon fillets, skin     just a little.
removed
                                     Place the salmon fillets onto the planks and discard the marinade.

                    Grilled Salmon with Curried Peach Sauce
Ingredients                         Directions
2 fresh peaches, peeled and diced Preheat an outdoor grill for medium-high heat, and lightly oil grate.
1/4 cup honey
1 teaspoon curry powder           Stir together the peaches, honey, and curry powder in a small
salt and pepper to taste          saucepan over medium heat. Bring to a simmer, and cook until the
2 salmon steaks                   peaches break down, and the sauce thickens, about 10 minutes.
                                  Season to taste with salt and pepper.

                                    Season the salmon steaks with salt and pepper, and cook on the
                                    preheated grill until the fish flakes easily with a fork, 5 to 10 minutes
                                    per side depending on the thickness of the steaks. Pour the peach
                                    sauce over the salmon to serve.

                               between an 1/8th to a 1/4 of an inch thick, and cover the surface of
                               the fish.

                               Place fish under the broiler about nine inches from heat source.
                               Broil for 8 to 10 minutes per inch of thickness, or until fish flakes
                               and flesh is opaque. Pesto should have formed a heavily browned
                               crust. Remove from the oven, and set aside for a few minutes.
                               Squeeze half of second lemon over fish. Slice remaining lemon half
                               into thin slices. Place lemon slices on individual servings, or
                               arrange on the whole flank if serving at the table.

Salt and pepper to taste              medium-sized saucepan should do. Pour lemon juice and olive oil

salmon fillets                        3/4-inch. Season the water to taste with salt and pepper, then add
                                      the salmon. Pour in enough water to just cover the salmon.
3 egg yolks
                                      Place the salmon over medium-high heat, and heat until the water is
1 tablespoon hot water
                                      hot and steaming, but not simmering (165 degrees F (75 degrees
1 cup butter, cut into small pieces
                                      C)); reduce heat if needed to keep the water at the proper
2 tablespoons fresh lemon juice       temperature. Poach until the salmon turns opaque, and is firm to the
Salt and pepper to taste              touch; or to an internal temperature of 140 degrees F (60 degrees
2 tablespoons chopped fresh           C). Proceed making the Hollandaise sauce while the salmon is
chives                                poaching.

                                      Pour a few inches of water into a saucepan, and bring to a boil over
                                      high heat, then turn heat to medium-high and maintain water at a
                                      gentle boil. Place the egg yolks in a metal bowl, and whisk in hot
                                      water. Place the bowl over, but not touching, the boiling water.
                                      Whisk constantly until the yolks thicken, turn a light, opaque yellow,
                                      and double in volume. You may need to remove the bowl from the
                                      boiling water from time to time in order to keep from scrambling the
                                      yolks.

                                      When the yolks have thickened and increased in volume, begin
                                      whisking in the butter, a cube at a time until it melts and
                                      incorporates into the hollandaise sauce. Wait for each cube to melt
                                      and incorporate before adding the next. Once all of the butter has
                                      been incorporated, remove from heat, whisk in the lemon juice, and
                                      season to taste with salt and pepper.

                                      To serve, drain the poached salmon and place each piece onto a
                                      dinner plate. Generously ladle with the warm hollandaise sauce, and
                                      sprinkle with chives.

                                     to be flaked with a fork. You may need to open one of the packets
                                     to check. To serve, place packets onto serving plates and use
                                     scissors to cut an X in the center, being careful not to cut the food.
